import 'dotenv/config'; import express from 'express'; import cors from 'cors'; import helmet from 'helmet'; import pg from 'pg'; import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs'; import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken'; const {Pool}=pg, app=express(); const PORT=Number(process.env.PORT||3000), JWT_SECRET=process.env.JWT_SECRET; if(!process.env.DATABASE_URL)throw new Error('Configure DATABASE_URL no .env'); if(!JWT_SECRET||JWT_SECRET.length<32)throw new Error('JWT_SECRET precisa ter ao menos 32 caracteres.'); const pool=new Pool({connectionString:process.env.DATABASE_URL,ssl:process.env.DATABASE_SSL==='false'?false:{rejectUnauthorized:false},max:10,idleTimeoutMillis:30000,connectionTimeoutMillis:10000}); app.use(helmet({contentSecurityPolicy:false}));app.use(cors({origin:process.env.CORS_ORIGIN||false}));app.use(express.json({limit:'1mb'})); const TABLES={ colaboradores:new Set(['id','nome','escala','dsr','horario','codigo','matricula','codigo_spx','email','ops_id','cargo','funcao','contrato','station','hub','sub_regional','spx','ativo','created_at','updated_at']), presenca_diaria:new Set(['id','colaborador_id','data','status','created_at','updated_at']), historico_escala:new Set(['id','colaborador_id','data_inicio','data_fim','setor','funcao','hora_inicio','hora_fim','created_at','updated_at']), logirotator_diaristas_diarios:new Set(['data','pedidos','presentes','faltantes','updated_by','updated_at']), logirotator_perfis:new Set(['user_id','nome','email','papel','ativo','created_at','updated_at']) }; const authRequired=(req,res,next)=>{try{const t=(req.headers.authorization||'').replace(/^Bearer\\s+/i,'');if(!t)return res.status(401).json({error:'Faça login para continuar.'});req.user=jwt.verify(t,JWT_SECRET);next();}catch{return res.status(401).json({error:'Sessão inválida ou expirada.'});}}; const adminRequired=(req,res,next)=>req.user?.papel==='admin'?next():res.status(403).json({error:'Ação permitida somente ao administrador.'}); const issueToken=u=>jwt.sign({id:u.user_id,email:u.email,papel:u.papel},JWT_SECRET,{expiresIn:'12h'}); const publicProfile=p=>({user_id:p.user_id,nome:p.nome,email:p.email,papel:p.papel,ativo:p.ativo}); app.get('/api/health',async(_req,res)=>{try{const q=await pool.query('SELECT NOW() AS database_time');res.json({ok:true,database:'Neon PostgreSQL',database_time:q.rows[0].database_time});}catch(e){res.status(503).json({ok:false,error:'Não foi possível conectar ao Neon.',detail:process.env.NODE_ENV==='development'?e.message:undefined});}}); app.post('/api/auth/login',async(req,res)=>{try{const email=String(req.body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase(),password=String(req.body.password||'');const q=await pool.query(`SELECT p.user_id,p.nome,p.email,p.papel,p.ativo,u.password_hash FROM logirotator_perfis p JOIN app_users u ON u.id=p.user_id WHERE lower(p.email)=lower($1) LIMIT 1`,[email]);const u=q.rows[0];if(!u||!u.ativo||!(await bcrypt.compare(password,u.password_hash)))return res.status(401).json({error:'E-mail ou senha inválidos.'});res.json({token:issueToken(u),user:publicProfile(u)});}catch(e){res.status(500).json({error:'Falha no login.',detail:process.env.NODE_ENV==='development'?e.message:undefined});}}); app.get('/api/auth/me',authRequired,async(req,res)=>{const q=await pool.query('SELECT user_id,nome,email,papel,ativo FROM logirotator_perfis WHERE user_id=$1',[req.user.id]);if(!q.rows[0]||!q.rows[0].ativo)return res.status(401).json({error:'Usuário inativo.'});res.json({user:publicProfile(q.rows[0])});}); app.post('/api/auth/users',authRequired,adminRequired,async(req,res)=>{const nome=String(req.body.nome||'').trim(),email=String(req.body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase(),password=String(req.body.password||''),papel=req.body.papel==='admin'?'admin':'usuario';if(nome.length<3||!email.includes('@')||password.length<8)return res.status(400).json({error:'Informe nome, e-mail válido e senha com pelo menos 8 caracteres.'});const c=await pool.connect();try{await c.query('BEGIN');const hash=await bcrypt.hash(password,12);const u=await c.query('INSERT INTO app_users(password_hash) VALUES($1) RETURNING id',[hash]);const p=await c.query('INSERT INTO logirotator_perfis(user_id,nome,email,papel,ativo) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,true) RETURNING user_id,nome,email,papel,ativo',[u.rows[0].id,nome,email,papel]);await c.query('COMMIT');res.status(201).json({user:publicProfile(p.rows[0])});}catch(e){await c.query('ROLLBACK');res.status(400).json({error:e.code==='23505'?'Já existe uma conta com este e-mail.':e.message});}finally{c.release();}}); app.post('/api/db/:table',authRequired,async(req,res)=>{const table=req.params.table,cols=TABLES[table];if(!cols)return res.status(404).json({error:'Tabela não permitida.'});const {action='select',columns='*',payload,filters=[],orders=[],limit,range,conflict}=req.body||{};if(action!=='select'&&req.user.papel!=='admin')return res.status(403).json({error:'Somente administrador pode alterar dados.'});const valid=c=>typeof c==='string'&&/^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(c)&&cols.has(c),params=[],bind=v=>{params.push(v);return `$${params.length}`;};try{let sql='';if(action==='select'){const cc=columns==='*'?'*':String(columns).split(',').map(x=>x.trim());if(cc.some(x=>!valid(x)))return res.status(400).json({error:'Coluna inválida.'});sql=`SELECT ${cc.join(',')} FROM ${table}`;}else if(action==='insert'||action==='upsert'){const rows=Array.isArray(payload)?payload:[payload];if(!rows.length||rows.some(x=>!x||typeof x!=='object'))return res.status(400).json({error:'Dados inválidos.'});const keys=[...new Set(rows.flatMap(x=>Object.keys(x)))];if(!keys.length||keys.some(k=>!valid(k)))return res.status(400).json({error:'Campo não permitido.'});sql=`INSERT INTO ${table} (${keys.join(',')}) VALUES ${rows.map(row=>'('+keys.map(k=>bind(row[k]??null)).join(',')+')').join(',')}`;if(action==='upsert'){const cc=String(conflict||'').split(',').filter(Boolean);if(!cc.length||cc.some(k=>!valid(k)))return res.status(400).json({error:'Chave de conflito inválida.'});const upd=keys.filter(k=>!cc.includes(k));sql+=` ON CONFLICT (${cc.join(',')}) `+(upd.length?`DO UPDATE SET ${upd.map(k=>`${k}=EXCLUDED.${k}`).join(',')}`:'DO NOTHING');}sql+=' RETURNING *';}else if(action==='update'){if(!payload||typeof payload!=='object')return res.status(400).json({error:'Dados inválidos.'});const keys=Object.keys(payload);if(!keys.length||keys.some(k=>!valid(k)))return res.status(400).json({error:'Campo não permitido.'});sql=`UPDATE ${table} SET ${keys.map(k=>`${k}=${bind(payload[k])}`).join(',')}`;}else if(action==='delete')sql=`DELETE FROM ${table}`;else return res.status(400).json({error:'Operação inválida.'}); const where=[];const effectiveFilters=[...filters];if(table==='logirotator_perfis'&&req.user.papel!=='admin')effectiveFilters.push({op:'eq',column:'user_id',value:req.user.id});for(const f of effectiveFilters){if(!valid(f.column))return res.status(400).json({error:'Filtro inválido.'});const c=f.column;if(f.op==='eq')where.push(`${c} IS NOT DISTINCT FROM ${bind(f.value)}`);else if(f.op==='neq')where.push(`${c} IS DISTINCT FROM ${bind(f.value)}`);else if(f.op==='gte')where.push(`${c} >= ${bind(f.value)}`);else if(f.op==='lte')where.push(`${c} <= ${bind(f.value)}`);else if(f.op==='in')where.push(Array.isArray(f.value)&&f.value.length?`${c}=ANY(${bind(f.value)})`:'FALSE');else if(f.op==='is')where.push(f.value===null?`${c} IS NULL`:`${c} IS ${f.value===true?'TRUE':'FALSE'}`);else if(f.op==='not'&&f.operator==='is'&&f.value===null)where.push(`${c} IS NOT NULL`);else return res.status(400).json({error:'Operador não permitido.'});}if(where.length)sql+=' WHERE '+where.join(' AND ');if(action==='select'){if(orders.some(o=>!valid(o.column)))return res.status(400).json({error:'Ordenação inválida.'});if(orders.length)sql+=' ORDER BY '+orders.map(o=>`${o.column} ${o.ascending===false?'DESC':'ASC'}`).join(',');if(Number.isInteger(limit)&&limit>=0)sql+=' LIMIT '+Math.min(limit,5000);if(Array.isArray(range)&&range.length===2){const off=Math.max(0,Number(range[0])||0),end=Math.max(off,Number(range[1])||off);sql+=` LIMIT ${Math.min(end-off+1,5000)} OFFSET ${off}`;}}else if(action==='update'||action==='delete')sql+=' RETURNING *';const q=await pool.query(sql,params);res.json({data:q.rows,count:q.rowCount});}catch(e){console.error('DB API:',e);res.status(400).json({error:e.code==='23505'?'Registro duplicado.':e.code==='23503'?'Registro relacionado não encontrado.':e.message});}}); const HTML_BASE64 = 'PCFET0NUWVBFIGh0bWw+CjxodG1sIGxhbmc9InB0LVBUIj4KPGhlYWQ+CjxtZXRhIGNoYXJzZXQ9IlVURi04Ij4KPG1ldGEgbmFtZT0idmlld3BvcnQiIGNvbnRlbnQ9IndpZHRoPWRldmljZS13aWR0aCxpbml0aWFsLXNjYWxlPTEsbWF4aW11bS1zY2FsZT0xLHVzZXItc2NhbGFibGU9bm8iPgo8bWV0YSBuYW1lPSJtb2JpbGUtd2ViLWFwcC1jYXBhYmxlIiBjb250ZW50PSJ5ZXMiPgo8dGl0bGU+TG9naVJvdGF0b3IgLSBIVUItTENFLTA0IEp1YXplaXJvIGRvIE5vcnRlPC90aXRsZT4KPG1ldGEgbmFtZT0idGhlbWUtY29sb3IiIGNvbnRlbnQ9IiMwOTA5MGIiPgo8bWV0YSBuYW1lPSJhcHBsZS1tb2JpbGUtd2ViLWFwcC1jYXBhYmxlIiBjb250ZW50PSJ5ZXMiPgo8bWV0YSBuYW1lPSJhcHBsZS1tb2JpbGUtd2ViLWFwcC1zdGF0dXMtYmFyLXN0eWxlIiBjb250ZW50PSJibGFjay10cmFuc2x1Y2VudCI+CjxsaW5rIHJlbD0iaWNvbiIgdHlwZT0iaW1hZ2Uvc3ZnK3htbCIgaHJlZj0iZGF0YTppbWFnZS9zdmcreG1sLDxzdmcgeG1sbnM9J2h0dHA6Ly93d3cudzMub3JnLzIwMDAvc3ZnJyB2aWV3Qm94PScwIDAgNTEyIDUxMic+PHJlY3Qgd2lkdGg9JzUxMicgaGVpZ2h0PSc1MTInIHJ4PScxMjAnIGZpbGw9JyUyM2Y1OWUwYicvPjxwYXRoIGQ9J00xMzAgMTgwIEw2MCAyNTYgTDEzMCAzMzIgTTM4MiAxODAgTDQ1MiAyNTYgTDM4MiAzMzIgTTI3NSAxNDAgTDIzNSAzNzInIHN0cm9rZT0nJTIzMDkwOTBiJyBzdHJva2Utd2lkdGg9JzQ4JyBzdHJva2UtbGluZWNhcD0ncm91bmQnIHN0cm9rZS1saW5lam9pbj0ncm91bmQnIGZpbGw9J25vbmUnLz48L3N2Zz4iPgo8c2NyaXB0IHNyYz0iaHR0cHM6Ly9jZG4udGFpbHdpbmRjc3MuY29tIj48L3NjcmlwdD4KPHNjcmlwdCBzcmM9Imh0dHBzOi8vdW5wa2cuY29tL2x1Y2lkZUBsYXRlc3QiPjwvc2NyaXB0Pgo8c3R5bGU+Cjpyb290ey0tcHJpbWFyeTozOCA5MiUgNTAlOy0tcHJpbWFyeS1mZzoyMjIgNDclIDQlOy0tc2Vjb25kYXJ5OjIxNyAzMyUgMTclOy0tYm9yZGVyOjIxNyAzMyUgMjAlOy0tbXV0ZWQ6MjE3IDMzJSAxNSU7LS1iZzoyMjIgNDclIDQlfQoqe2JveC1zaXppbmc6Ym9yZGVyLWJveH0KYm9keXtmb250LWZlYXR1cmUtc2V0dGluZ3M6ImN2MTEiLCJzczAxIjstd2Via2l0LWZvbnQtc21vb3RoaW5nOmFudGlhbGlhc2VkfQouYnRue2Rpc3BsYXk6aW5saW5lLWZsZXg7YWxpZ24taXRlbXM6Y2VudGVyO2p1c3RpZnktY29udGVudDpjZW50ZXI7Z2FwOi41cmVtO3doaXRlLXNwYWNlOm5vd3JhcDtib3JkZXItcmFkaXVzOi41cmVtO2ZvbnQtc2l6ZTouODEyNXJlbTtmb250LXdlaWdodDo2MDA7aGVpZ2h0OjIuMjVyZW07cGFkZGluZzowIC44NzVyZW07Ym9yZGVyOjFweCBzb2xpZCB0cmFuc3BhcmVudDt0cmFuc2l0aW9uOmFsbCAuMTVzIGVhc2U7Y3Vyc29yOnBvaW50ZXI7dXNlci1zZWxlY3Q6bm9uZTtvdXRsaW5lOm5vbmV9Ci5idG46Zm9jdXMtdmlzaWJsZXtvdXRsaW5lOjJweCBzb2xpZCB app.get('/',(_req,res)=>res.type('html').send(Buffer.from(HTML_BASE64,'base64').toString('utf8'))); async function bootstrapAdmin(){ const email=String(process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL||'').trim().toLowerCase(); const password=String(process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD||''); const nome=String(process.env.ADMIN_NAME||'Administrador').trim(); if(!email || !password) return; if(password.length<8) throw new Error('ADMIN_PASSWORD precisa ter ao menos 8 caracteres.'); const found=await pool.query("SELECT 1 FROM logirotator_perfis WHERE papel='admin' LIMIT 1"); if(found.rowCount) return; const client=await pool.connect(); try{ await client.query('BEGIN'); const hash=await bcrypt.hash(password,12); const u=await client.query('INSERT INTO app_users(password_hash) VALUES($1) RETURNING id',[hash]); await client.query("INSERT INTO logirotator_perfis(user_id,nome,email,papel,ativo) VALUES($1,$2,$3,'admin',true)",[u.rows[0].id,nome,email]); await client.query('COMMIT'); console.log('Administrador inicial criado:',email); }catch(e){await client.query('ROLLBACK');throw e;}finally{client.release();} } bootstrapAdmin().then(()=>app.listen(PORT,()=>console.log(`LogiRotator em http://localhost:${PORT}`))).catch(e=>{console.error('Falha ao iniciar:',e.message);process.exit(1);});
